# W1 combined owner walkthrough (draft written at W1-UX-05 closeout, 2026-10-17)

Source: umbrella `tasks/W1-UX-mockup-fidelity.md` "Combined walkthrough coverage and authorship". Owner: whichever UX slice merges last. UX-05 wrote this draft; if UX-02, 03 or 04 merges later, that slice refreshes its rows and captures. The coordinator checks all 26 rows before scheduling the owner session.

## How to run it

- Serve one tool at a time; never serve two at once.
- Substitute only the IDs and selectors that the tool's `links`/`serve` output prints.
- Compare the app with `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html#/<id>` at 1440 and 390 px. Check keyboard access, the exact links and a reload.
- Drop each guarded case afterwards.
- Do not compare numbers across cases.

All data is authored or generated test data, not field equipment readings. Label it that way in the guide and in every screenshot.

### Prerequisites

1. Postgres must be running on 127.0.0.1:55432 (`ASSETOPS_PG_PASSWORD`).
2. Copy the Kobo site file into this worktree: `var/sites/kobo-001.yaml`, taken from the main checkout (`var/` is ignored).
3. Run `python tools/s4_finding_cases.py run` once. It produces the `assetops_s4cases` database and manifest that `v01a`, `v01b` and the S4 tools copy.
4. `s2_demo_walkthrough.py` has `run|serve|setup`, with no `prepare` mode.
   - Use `run`, then `serve`.
   - `run` recreates the fixed database `assetops_s2demo`; drop it afterwards, because the tool has no drop mode.
5. `s5_overview_walkthrough.py prepare` needs `var/sites/kobo-001.yaml`. Run `tick` within 5 minutes before viewing.
6. **Frontend for each tool:** from `frontend/`, set `$env:ASSETOPS_API_PORT="<api>"` and run `npm.cmd run dev -- --port <web> --strictPort`.
   - Vite listens on `localhost`, not `127.0.0.1`.
   - Open the printed path on `http://localhost:<web>`.
7. **Captures:** `node tools/w1-ux-05-evidence.mjs <out> <mock-id>[:<case>]=<url> ...` (or the UX-01, 02, 03 or 04 sibling script for that slice's cards).

# Site-first task queue

**Current direction (2026-10-07):** [current system architecture](../frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp.md), [current technical design](../frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md), and [Site-first execution plan](../frontend/docs/architecture/site-first-execution-plan.md). These are current implementation authority under project routing; their proposal headers do not record owner sign-off. The Architect decision `D-2026-10-07-s1-foundation-import-and-cutover` and S1-01 addendum control the first slice. This is the only executable queue. The [superseded T0xx queue](archive/pre-site-first-2026-10-07/README.md) and its open packets are historical reference with original status intact. Completed tasks and decisions remain historical built-state evidence.

One mini-grid Site and one assessed window form the first end-to-end path. S1-01/02/03 and S2-01a/b/c/S2-02 are reviewed and merged through `82ba6cd`; the owner passed the S2 Evidence checkpoint and adopted V-01 on 2026-10-09. The owner confirmed W1's revised 27-screen target, core panels, demo values and K=10 on 2026-10-11 (`.agent/owner-W1-confirmation-2026-10-11.md`). S3â€“S7 remain outlines. Startup guards and S3 dispatch contract/fixtures remain activation gates. S2-02r is a follow-up before unattended/multi-operator rotation or a real pilot, not a dispatch prerequisite.

For implementation, read the active packet and the contract sections it names. The registry, ledger and follow-on lanes below guide Architect/Planner review; they do not expand an active packet's scope.

**Pre-S3 planning gates:** `A-claim-work-routes`, `A-actor-identity` and bounded `A-evidence-readiness-health` are recorded in the pre-S3 decisions. `A-finding-index` is resolved by `D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index`; S4-04 implementation and its fixtures remain open. `A-money-comparability` and broad shared projections remain open. S4-01 and S4-02 consume the route and actor decisions; S6-01 reuses both accepted contracts. These decisions do not add implementation prerequisites to dispatch. At each S2â€“S7 outline refinement, compare the packet with the registry's `queue_baseline_nodes`; a contract or fixture no longer owned by the queue leaves that baseline before marginal estimates are used.

**Simulation and edge decision:** `D-2026-10-07-s2-edge-split-and-demo-source` sets S2-01a/b/c and a simulated-only S2 demo. S2-02 owns authenticated upload and central diagnostics; the first real pilot chooses a real capture or labelled vendor-format fixture for physical protocol proof. `A-edge-batch-signature` defers batch signatures/key rotation; TLS gateway credentials, canonical digest and exact-byte retry remain required. S3-01 owns dispatch scenarios, S6-02 owns two-run verification scenarios, and causal battery-stress production remains outside S7. At each outline refinement, the Planner checks `queue_baseline_nodes` against the refined packet; technical `requires` remain separate from queue order.

The recorded `A-lab-registered-basis`, `A-edge-diagnostics` and `A-demo-scenarios` contracts are consumed by S2-01a/c and later packets. S2-01c owns `I-edge-fault-scenarios`; S3-01 owns `I-sim-dispatch-cases`; S6-02 owns `I-sim-verification-cases`. `A-real-peer-source` records simulated-only demo input; the first real pilot records its physical peer before refining an adapter task.

G-03â€“G-05 wait for their reviewed merged UI and packet prerequisites; run at the first free slot with no ready active-wave core candidate, or take their remaining backlog to W1-exit review. A â‰¤5% ranking contest still goes to the owner before activating a due G packet; a 5â€“10% notice is recorded. This ordering needs no new owner choice and gives no G node a demo value. The screen ledger's `gap_backfill_task` is a planning pointer, not a delivered gap ID. G-01 introduces the catalogue schema and one real tagged Readiness gateway-firmware missing state; G-02 enrolls the four V-01a screens for complete non-core/open `gap_ids` coverage, then later batches enroll their merged UI.

## Screen-outward planning gate

The [v6.9 screen realization map](../frontend/docs/architecture/v69-screen-realization-map.md), [screen ledger](../frontend/docs/architecture/v69-screen-ledger.json), [model homes](../frontend/docs/architecture/v69-model-homes.json) and [panel contracts](../frontend/docs/architecture/v69-panel-contracts.json) are Architect planning overlays, not accepted screen completion. They account for 110 manifest IDs, 88 model names and 258 visible panels. The owner approved `Real with honest gaps` as the W1 target for 27 screens, including six moved from W4, and their exact core keys/values; later-wave tiers remain pending. The manifest ID and its `visual_reference` key each later comparison; `GAP` in a panel contract means no supported claim yet. Run `python frontend/docs/architecture/check_v69_ledger.py` after ledger changes.

For each `Deferred` screen, the ledger's `promotion_dependencies.to_candidate` lists the design, projection, fixture and task gates before it can enter the executable queue. For every proposed `Real with honest gaps` screen, `promotion_dependencies.to_real` names unresolved panels and remaining work before full mock fidelity can be claimed. A named S task alone does not satisfy that gate. The Planner checks these fields when refining a packet and updates them when a dependency is met or the owner changes a tier.

S1 and S2 are merged; W1 owner input and `A-S3-dispatch` are recorded. **Before S3-01 is activated**, pass PRE-S3-03/startup guards and check the split [V-01 umbrella](V-01-v69-visual-fidelity.md). S3-01 contains the versioned `I-sim-dispatch-cases` fixture as phase 1, followed by typed assessment and UI/cutover phases; its Candidate path stays Indeterminate until the new capability channels are accepted. S3-02 owns the later positive Evidence Analysis claim-limit view. S4-03 owns Finding Evidence/History; S4-04 owns the Site Finding list. S5-01 owns selected-window context/timeline/money; S5-02 integrates the list with separate empty and positive fixtures. S6-01 owns Action detail/activity/change, S6-02 owns plan/evidence/outcome, and S6-03 owns the Site Work list. Resolve only model gaps merited by the next supported claim; broader work, domains and indexes stay in later waves. A target tier does not authorize an empty route or illustrative mock value as product data.

S2-02 refinement compared `queue_baseline_nodes`: `I-accepted-evidence-store`, `I-edge-diagnostics-store` and `I-S2-02` remain in S2-02; S2-01b/c retain their nodes. None leaves the baseline. This is a cutover map, not permission to delete historical fixtures or completed task evidence. The technical design's demo-to-Postgres comparison must pass per supported read: Evidence changes in S2-02 after evidence comparison; Performance changes in S3-01 after dispatch comparison. S2-01b makes the gateway durable, S2-01c exposes diagnostics/faults, and S2-02 makes upload and central acceptance real; S3 consumes durable recompute jobs. Do not call the current in-process publisher or JSON ingestion the production gateway/ledger.

All packets inherit the current architecture and technical design. # S7-01b review packet - Site Health and battery boundary UI

Task `tasks/S7-01b-health-ui.md`; branch `task/S7-01b-health-ui` from main `f3de235a`, which already contains S7-01a. USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true. ## Outcome

- **Site Health** at `/sites/{site}/health`. The Health tab is now enabled.
  - The status strip is labelled "Gateway contact" and gives the enabled-gateway count. Disabled gateways are shown as "N disabled, not counted".
  - Separately qualified sections follow, in v69 `#/health` order: Battery condition; Gateway contact (server receipt against the declared interval); Queue and storage, as last reported; Device contact reported by gateways.
  - A closing notice says that contact is not equipment condition.
  - Technical details are closed by default and hold the IDs, rule, snapshot, watermark digest and reason codes.
  - `?gateway=<gateway id>` selects one gateway. The selection shows its latest report ID and sequence, and it survives reload.
- **Battery** pages:
  - `/sites/{site}/assets` is the entry. It lists the configured battery, or shows "No battery configured" with no asset ID.
  - `/sites/{site}/assets/{asset id}` is the detail page. Condition is Unknown, with the three named missing inputs. The declared rating is labelled "not a measurement". State of charge is "Not shown". Signal channels show as connected, none, or not set up. Withheld items are listed. Asset, Foundation, channel-set and channel revision IDs are in disclosures.
  - An asset ID outside the effective basis returns a typed refusal (404) and no condition row. An invalid ID returns 422. A 401, 403, 409 or 503 is a typed refusal that keeps its HTTP status and code.
- **S7-01a backend follow-up** (`D-2026-10-16-s701-clock`):
  - Separate constants `CLOCK_OFFSET_LIMIT_MS` and `OBSERVED_AFTER_RECEIPT_ALLOWANCE_MS`, both 5000 ms and inclusive. The second is now disclosed in the read as `clock.observed_after_receipt_allowance_ms`.
  - `summary.reason` is `NO_ENABLED_GATEWAY` or `NO_REGISTERED_GATEWAY`.
  - Ages are null under an UNTRUSTED clock, and for any time later than `read_at`.
  - Review L6 is done: a binding set that names another Foundation revision returns 409 `FOUNDATION_BASIS_UNAVAILABLE`.
- **Walkthrough** `tools/s701_health_walkthrough.py prepare|serve|links|deliver|attest|drop` (review L1 and L4):
  - It uses a private DB.
  - It creates the KOBO-001 bindings before registering the case gateways.
  - `deliver` refreshes all six cases meant to read CURRENT.
  - `attest` asserts the stale-cadence case only after 60 s.
  - KOBO-002 is a second Site built from the Kobo file without its battery.
  - `serve` prints the exact URLs.

# W1-UX-05 review: Foundation, Readiness, SLD, Health and battery presentation

Reviewer: a fresh, independent Claude reviewer. I did not author this slice and did not edit it.
Revision: `35c1adf1` (detached, worktree `AssetOpsV1-gap-planning`), base `815888f0`.
Diff: `git diff 815888f0..35c1adf1 -- ':!.agent'` (26 files). The changes after `af9b6406` are docs only, so the implementer's full-suite run at `af9b6406` covers the code under review.

## Verdict: ACCEPT

I found no High or Medium findings. The eight Lows below do not block the merge. Finding 1 must be fixed before anyone relies on 390 px measurements in the combined W1 walkthrough.

## Honest state: what I checked

- **Unknown, stale, refused and not configured never read as positive.**
  - No new `ProductWords` uses `positive`. Foundation period words are `neutral`, `waiting` or `limited`. Set-up readiness, Health contact and battery condition are the same.
  - Codes the screen does not recognise fall back to `limited` with `UNRECOGNISED_SENTENCE`.
  - See `foundationHeadlines.tsx:39-127` and `healthHeadlines.tsx:31-126,243-262`.
- **Commissioning confirmed.** It is always `not-known` ("Not recorded here", `foundationHeadlines.tsx:294-298`). The headline always adds "commissioning is not yet confirmed".
- **Set up to report.** It is `neutral` and says it "does not mean any device is connected, reporting or healthy" (`foundationHeadlines.tsx:91-99`). The test asserts the tone (`siteFoundationRevision.test.tsx:2121`).
- **Reporting now.** It is passed as `value: null` (`foundationHeadlines.tsx:394`). `ValueBars` draws that as a hatched "not known" mark, never a fill. Tested at `siteFoundationRevision.test.tsx:2172-2175`.
- **Fresh contact with old facts.** It is in the attention list: `contact !== CURRENT || factAgeUnknown || reported_facts.status !== CURRENT` (`healthHeadlines.tsx:162-164`). Tested with `s7-delayed-old` showing "in contact; its latest report is old".
- **Battery condition is never judged.**
  - The headline is fixed: "Battery condition: cannot yet be judged".
  - `isBatteryDetail` now refuses any condition other than UNKNOWN (`healthClient.ts:211-213`), so a "GOOD" value becomes a refusal panel, not a card. This is tested.
  - No remaining life, warranty or replacement wording appears.
- **The state line stays outside the closed panel.**
  - `ProductStatus` renders in the card's status slot, before the visual and outside `ExactReferences`.
  - `expectStateLineVisible` runs on every card state tested.
  - Refusals and the unreadable store have no read instant. They use a local equivalent that checks the same visibility (`siteHealthBoundary.test.tsx:410-421`).

**Changed test assertions.** I diffed every removed line in the four test files. All are wording-only renames with the same selector, role and count:

- binding â†’ channel link
- PV array / Load â†’ Solar array / Supplied users
- Minimum generator runtime â†’ Shortest generator operation
- Critical-load priority â†’ Critical service order
- claim readiness â†’ what the readings can support
- claims â†’ says
- firmware "runs" â†’ "has installed": same gap ID and placement

One assertion changed source as well as wording. `siteFoundationRevision.test.tsx:327` used to check the backend sentence and now checks the frontend constant that replaces it. That is still an exact full-sentence match, and the next two assertions (`data-readiness-state` UNKNOWN, registration-is-not-contact) are unchanged.

`siteHealthBoundary.test.tsx` is purely additive apart from one import. No fail-closed assertion was removed or loosened: "Not bound", "Unknown until bound", not-current, refusal, pending-gateway and no-healthy all remain. I confirm the packet's claim.

## Findings

1. **Low (blocks the combined walkthrough evidence): the 390 px measurements in `w1-ux-01-evidence.mjs` and `w1-ux-05-evidence.mjs` cannot fail. Treat every "narrow" measured claim as not established.**
   - **Where.** `tools/w1-ux-05-evidence.mjs:98-101,130,141,186`, which is identical to `tools/w1-ux-01-evidence.mjs:98-101,130,141,186`. I diffed lines 90-145 of both and they match.
   - **What is wrong.**
     - The claim is `scrollWidth <= clientWidth && wide.length === 0`. It never checks that `clientWidth` is near the requested width.
     - The UX-05 run recorded `clientWidth` 1425 at both widths. That is 1440 minus a 15 px classic scrollbar, so the page was laid out at desktop width when measured.
     - A mobile 390 override would show about 390 with no scrollbar. So the measurement ran without the override in effect.
     - `shot()` reapplies the override immediately before capture, which explains why the PNGs are 390 px wide.
   - **Consequence.**
     - Every measured "narrow" claim was effectively taken at desktop width: no sideways scroll, state and as-of boxes drawn, and Tab reach.
     - UX-01's packet records "390 scrollWidth = clientWidth" as met but gives no value. It ran the same code, so that pass is unproven: not shown false, but not evidence.
     - UX-05 records this honestly ("not established by measurement", visual check only), so it is not a packet-integrity finding against this slice.
   - **Fix, in the shared script pattern (UX-01 owner or coordinator):**
     - After `size(width, height)` and `go(...)`, wait with `waitFor(\`window.innerWidth === ${width} && document.documentElement.clientWidth <= ${width}\`)`.
     - Add a claim that records `innerWidth` and `clientWidth` and fails unless `clientWidth` is within `[width - 20, width]`. Measure only after that claim passes.
     - If the override still does not hold for the measurement, run each width in its own Chrome launched with `--window-size=<width>,<height>`, or use `Browser.getWindowForTarget` with `Browser.setWindowBounds`. Then capture with the override kept equal to that window size.
     - Rerun the narrow pass for UX-01 and UX-05, or have the coordinator's combined-walkthrough captures replace both.
     - `tools/v01a-foundation-evidence.mjs` uses the same `size()` pattern. Check its narrow `vw` readings too.

2. **Low: the shared helper `expectStateLineVisible` rejects the honest missing bound.**
   - **Where.** `frontend/src/test/productPresentation.ts` (`toMatch(/^As of /)`) against `frontend/src/ui/productWords.ts:399-402`. `asOfText(null)` returns "As of: time not recorded".
   - **Which side changes: the helper.** "As of: time not recorded" is the honest output for a read with no instant (refusals, an unreadable store) and must not become a time or "now".
   - **Fix.**
     - Change the helper to `/^As of (?:\d{1,2} [A-Z][a-z]{2} \d{4}, \d{2}:\d{2} UTC|: time not recorded)$/`.
     - Make the same change to the regex in the evidence scripts (`w1-ux-0x-evidence.mjs:184`).
     - Then replace UX-05's local `expectRefusedStateLine` (`siteHealthBoundary.test.tsx:410-421`) with the shared helper.
   - **Owner.** This is a UX-01 shared file, so the UX-01 owner or a W1 cleanup slice makes the change, not this branch.

3. **Low: the S1 "Ready to report" green badge under the neutral card.**
   - **Where.** `FoundationBindingsPanel.tsx:31-47,163`: `READINESS_WORD.READY_TO_REPORT = "Ready to report"`, tone `configured`, a green tint with a check mark.
   - **No semantic contradiction.**
     - The panel description says it is distinct from contact and from what the readings can support.
     - The verdict sentence is configuration-only.
     - The card directly above says "Set up to report â€¦ does not mean any device is connected, reporting or healthy".
   - **Where it is weak.** The same state carries two names a few lines apart, and one of them, "Ready to report" with a green check, reads more positively than the card allows.
   - **Recommendation.** Rename the badge to "Set up to report" in the W1 plain-language batch. That changes an S1-owned test, so route it through the Architect. The configured tone can stay.

4. **Low: Readiness shows "Gateway registered: Done" while contact is stale.**
   - **Where.** `foundationHeadlines.tsx:289-293`.
   - **Not misleading as worded.** The step is labelled "Gateway registered" with the detail "Registered; contact is reported below". The meaning block points contact and reporting to Health. The Gateway status panel below shows the stale contact.
   - **The gap.** The strip title "From configuration to confirmed reporting" invites reading the strip as progress toward reporting, and the card itself never surfaces stale contact.
   - **Recommendation for the owner walkthrough.** Either retitle the strip so it does not promise reporting (for example "Set-up steps"), or add a contact line when the gateway-health read is available. The card currently has no gateway-health input. This is not an acceptance gap: the packet asked for set-up, not contact.

5. **Low: two fallback headlines can sound positive when the code is not recognised.**
   - **Readiness.** When `readiness.state` is not recognised and no roles, gaps or gateways are missing, the headline reads "Nothing is missing from the set-upâ€¦" and "Readings linked" is `done` (`foundationHeadlines.tsx:286,312-317`). The state line beside it does say "Set-up state not recognised".
   - **Health.** When `summary.status` is not recognised and no gateway is enabled, the headline reads "All 0 enabled gateways are in contact and their latest reports are recent" (`healthHeadlines.tsx:165-169`).
   - **How likely.** Both are defensive edges the backend should not produce.
   - **Fix.** Branch the headline on `words.unrecognised`, and on `enabled.length === 0`, to a non-positive sentence.

6. **Low: the selected gateway's own state line says "In contact" for a gateway with old facts.**
   - **Where.** `SiteHealth.tsx:476-480`, using `gatewayContactWords`.
   - **What is wrong.** The scope says "contact" and the fact list beside it says the report is old. The panel's state line, though, does not carry the "needs attention" the headline list gives this gateway.
   - **Recommendation.** Have the line say "In contact; its latest report is old", or add a `next` that does.

7. **Low: S7-01b L2 is deferred correctly but must be tracked.**
   - **Where.** `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/source_health.py:258` sends `_seconds(d.received_at - d.observed_at)`, which goes negative under an untrusted clock.
   - **No frontend exposure.**
     - `SiteHealth.tsx:107` shows a lag only when it is 60 s or more.
     - An untrusted clock or an `OBSERVED_AFTER_RECEIPT` reason returns "Unknown age" before the lag is reached (`SiteHealth.tsx:104`).
     - No other frontend site renders the field.
   - **Fix.** A backend task nulls a negative `receipt_lag_seconds` (or nulls it whenever `trust == "UNTRUSTED"`) and adds a contract-fixture test. It is listed only in CODE_STATE "Open", so put it in the queue.

8. **Low: the backend "binding" sentences replaced on screen.**
   - **Where.** `FoundationBindingsPanel.tsx:96-101,196,271,372-374`.
   - **I checked the three replacements against the backend.**
     - `foundation_api.py:340-343` (NONE state)
     - `binding_service.py:773-775` (readiness statement)
     - `binding_service.py:935-939` (snapshot reason)
   - **Each is meaning-preserving.** It keeps "every role is unbound", "readiness is Unknown" and "operational status is not assessed". Each is keyed on the same condition the backend uses: no binding set, or `binding_set_revision_id === null`.
   - **Residual risk.** If the backend changes what it says in that condition, the UI will mask it.
   - **Fix.** Reword the backend sentences into product words, then remove the frontend overrides. Not needed for this merge.

No wording-preference findings are raised.

# W1-UX-04 review - Action, work, change and verification presentation

Reviewer: fresh independent Claude reviewer. I did not author this slice.
Revision reviewed: `56a63bb6` (build `de7d14ef` plus the closeout commit), detached in `AssetOpsV1-review4`.
Diff reviewed: `git diff b4a89314..56a63bb6 -- ':!.agent'`.

## Verdict: RETURN

The slice is mostly sound. The S6-02b fail-closed tests are intact, and most honest-state rules hold on the new cards. Three Medium findings block the merge:

- Finding 1: a selected historical outcome loses its source qualification and known failures on the Verification card.
- Finding 2: an INCONCLUSIVE outcome with a measured safety failure names the failure but does not show its value and limit.
- Finding 3: the packet records the 390 px measured claims as met, but the shared capture tool cannot measure at 390 px.

All three are small to fix.

## Honest state: what holds

- **A measured safety failure.** On a current INEFFECTIVE outcome with `GUARDRAIL_FAIL`, the label reads "Trial missed a safety or service limit", never "Target not met". The value and limit appear in the default reading ("measured 60 min, limit 0 min" in the served guardrail PNG). The source and the policy-trial / no-savings note sit beside them.
- **Current VERIFIED.** It keeps "within the sampled limits", the source label and the demonstration note.
- **REASSESSMENT_PENDING.** `showRecord` is false (`SiteActionVerification.tsx:487`). The card has no record, no bars and a waiting tone.
- **Historical selection.**
  - The card reads Historical with "Current state: ..." beside it.
  - `CurrentStrip` (Historical banner, actual current state) still renders above every card on Plan, Evidence and Verification.
  - The selector stays in the address.
- **"Applied".** It is "Applied; effect not yet measured", in a limited tone. The Change card is limited and never positive.
- **Site Work.**
  - The proved zero renders only for a loaded read with `total_count === 0`, with `read_at` as its as-of.
  - A refused or unread list shows no count, no bars and "time not recorded", never the current time.
  - Nothing is shown while the read is loading.
  - No verdict is derived.
- **RESOLVED and holds.** RESOLVED stays withdrawable in the unchanged S6 panels and in `TrialNotice`. The different-Finding hold is visible on the card, with a problem tone and a link to the blocking Action.
- **Default reading.** The state sentence, scope and as-of are outside the closed disclosure on every card. The tests assert this per state.

## Findings

### 1. Medium - a historical outcome on the Verification card drops its source label, demonstration note and known failures

`frontend/src/sites/verificationCards.tsx:101-112`

- **What is wrong.**
  - When `historical` is true, the card renders `ProductStatus` with `currentnessWords("HISTORICAL")` and a single line: "Outcome then: {label}. Current state: ...".
  - Unlike the current branch (`ProductOutcome`, `:114-120`), it never renders:
    - `words.source.label`
    - `words.demonstrationNote`
    - the `KNOWN_FAILURE` reasons
- **Effect.** In the builder's own capture (`s602b/app-verification-historical-narrow.png`), the card headline reads "An earlier outcome, shown as a record: target met within the sampled limits", with no source line. The source qualification ("Registered Lab trial data") appears only in the comparison table much further down. A historical INCONCLUSIVE would also drop its known target failure from the card.
- **Why it matters.** It breaks the rule that VERIFIED keeps its source label, and that known failures stay visible. The source qualification is defined as "always shown beside the outcome" (`productWords.ts` `SourceQualification`). This branch is exactly where an old positive is most likely to be misread.
- **Fix.**
  - In the historical branch, also render the source line and the demonstration note from `words`.
  - Render the known failures, for example by reusing the same children `ProductOutcome` builds.
  - Add an assertion to the existing "selected withdrawn outcome is Historical" test that the card contains `Source:` and the demonstration note.

### 2. Medium - INCONCLUSIVE with a measured safety failure: no value or limit on the card, and the known failure prints twice

`frontend/src/sites/verificationCards.tsx:93-94,114-120`; `frontend/src/ui/ProductStatus.tsx:85-105`; `frontend/src/sites/verificationWords.ts:121-122`

- **How the backend produces this case.** The evaluator returns INCONCLUSIVE with `KNOWN_FAILURE:below_reserve_seconds` whenever any fact is unknown while a guardrail was measured as FAIL (`backend/assetops_backend/actions/verification_rules.py:776-778`).
- **What the card does.** It computes `limits` for that case, but `ProductOutcome` renders limits only when `showsFailedLimit`, which is INEFFECTIVE with `GUARDRAIL_FAIL`. So the measured value and limit never appear. The failure shows only as a name, and its text doubles the prefix: `reasonWords` returns "Known failure: Time below reserve." and `ProductOutcome` adds another "Known failure: ".
- **Test gap.** The test titled "INCONCLUSIVE keeps known failures" (`siteActionVerification.test.tsx:599`) asserts only `/cannot tell|could not conclude/`. It never checks that a known failure is visible.
- **Why it matters.** It breaks two rules:
  - A measured safety or service failure must show its value and limit.
  - Unknown evidence must not hide known failures.

  This is the state the task singles out, and the served INCONCLUSIVE capture was not run, so nothing else caught it.
- **Fix.**
  - Render `limits` on the card whenever `limits.length > 0`, regardless of outcome. Either do it in the card's own children, or widen `showsFailedLimit` in the shared helper to cover INCONCLUSIVE with a measured guardrail FAIL. The shared helper is W1-UX-01 code; keep any change there additive.
  - Pass known failures without the duplicated prefix.
  - Extend the INCONCLUSIVE test case with a FAIL guardrail metric. Assert the "measured ... limit ..." text and the known-failure text in `[data-product-status]`.

### 3. Medium (packet integrity and acceptance evidence) - the 390 px claims were measured at desktop width; this slice neither fixed nor qualified that

`tools/w1-ux-01-evidence.mjs:98-101,186-201`; `.agent/W1-UX-04-review-packet.md:77,107-117`

- **The defect.** It was reported in the W1-UX-05 review, finding 1. `size()` sets the device-metrics override, but the measurement never checks that `innerWidth` or `clientWidth` is near 390. The UX-05 run recorded `clientWidth` 1425 at both widths. `shot()` reapplies the override before capture, so the PNGs are 390 px wide even though the measurement was not taken at 390.
- **What this slice changed.** Its tool edits (the card map, `W1_NO_VISUAL_OK`, `W1_TAB_TRACE`) do not touch this. The slice did not make the defect worse.
- **What the packet claims.** It records "Met: 364/364" for 1440/390, which presents the narrow sideways-scroll, state-box and Tab-reach claims for all nine screens as measured.
- **What the PNGs show.** The 390 Tab walk that reached `/sites/KOBO-002/health` is consistent with a desktop-width layout. The narrow PNGs I opened (Verification historical, Verification guardrail) look correct at 390. That is a visual check, not the measured claim.
- **What must happen.**
  1. This slice: the packet records the narrow measured claims as **not established by measurement** (visual check of the PNGs only), not as met.
  2. Before the combined W1 walkthrough relies on any 390 claim, the coordinator or the UX-01 owner fixes the shared tool:
     - After `size()` and `go()`, wait for `window.innerWidth === width`.
     - Add a claim that fails unless `clientWidth` is within `[width-20, width]`.
     - Rerun the narrow pass for UX-01, UX-04 and UX-05, or replace those passes with the combined walkthrough captures.

  The tool fix is not required on this branch. If the implementer makes it here, rerun the narrow pass and record the measured widths.
- **`W1_NO_VISUAL_OK`.** It can exempt any mock id from the "card has a visual" claim through an environment variable. The packet does not say which runs set it, or for which ids. Record that too.

### 4. Low - Evidence headline names only the later window when both windows are incomplete

`frontend/src/sites/verificationCards.tsx:321`

When both windows are incomplete, the headline says "Some readings are missing from the later window", which understates the gap. The bars beside it show both counts. Fix: name both windows when both are incomplete.

### 5. Low - Evidence and Plan cards on a cutoff selection do not carry the Historical label themselves

`verificationCards.tsx:200-226,299-333`

- **What happens.**
  - The Evidence card shows "Both windows have their readings" in a neutral tone, with a scope sentence saying "selected earlier time".
  - A cutoff-selected Plan that is still current reads "Plan fixed before the later readings".
- **What mitigates it.** The `CurrentStrip` directly above shows the Historical banner and the actual current state (F1). Neither card claims a verdict, so this is not blocking.
- **Optional fix.** Use `currentnessWords("HISTORICAL")` in these cards when `explicit` is set.

### 6. Low - the Action-work and Change step strips show the verification label with no source qualification

`frontend/src/sites/SiteAction.tsx:768`; `frontend/src/sites/SiteFoundationChange.tsx:392`

`outcomeWords(state, reasons, null)` gives the VERIFIED step the detail "Target met within the sampled limits" with no source. The tone downgrade only affects tone, which the step strip does not show. The W1-UX-01 Overview card already does the same (`SiteAction.tsx:392`). A related artefact: a Verification card with no record prints "Source: Where these readings came from is not established", because `null` is passed as the source.

These are summaries, and each links to Verification. Fix in the W1 copy pass: say "see Verification for its source", or pass the source when the read has it.

### 7. Low - one mock capture is blank but was counted as rendered

`%TEMP%\w1ux04-shots\s601b\mock-changes-narrow.png`

This 390 px capture is blank (2.9 KB). The "mock renders" claim passed anyway. The s602b run has a valid `mock-changes` capture, so the comparison exists. This shows the `h1, h2` readiness check is weak. Note it in the packet.

### 8. Low (user-review item, not a defect) - duplication

The new cards sit above the unchanged S6 panels. This repeats the outcome, the failed limits and the source (see the guardrail PNG: the card, then the Outcome panel, then the "Trial missed a safety or service limit" panel). The duplication is what keeps the S6-02b fail-closed tests exact, so it is the right trade for W1. The owner should judge the density in the combined walkthrough. Folding the panels belongs in a later slice with its own test migration, not this fix round.

### 9. Low - the two uncaptured S6-02b cases

`target_miss` and `inconclusive` were not served. That is recorded honestly as not run, with jsdom coverage. However, finding 2 is precisely a defect in the INCONCLUSIVE card that jsdom did not assert. In the fix round, run the `inconclusive` served case (one case at a time is fine) after finding 2 is fixed, or record it as not run again together with the new jsdom assertion.

## Residual risk not named in the packet

- **Known-failure prefix.** The doubled "Known failure:" prefix in `ProductOutcome` affects any other caller that passes `reasonWords` output.
- **Evidence received counts.** They count slots where `slotProblems` is empty. A slot with an unrecognised cell state but no listed problem would count as received. The `complete` flag from the read still drives the state line, so the label cannot read complete falsely.
- **Recommendation as-of.** The "as of" for an available recommendation is the latest input window end. That shows how old the inputs are, not when the page was read.

# W1-UX-03 review: Evidence, Evidence Analysis, Dispatch and Analysis presentation

Reviewer: fresh, independent Claude reviewer. I did not author this slice.
Revision reviewed: `c5627f21` (detached), diff `git diff b4a89314..c5627f21 -- ':!.agent'`.
Inputs read: the task packet, umbrella, UX-01 handoff, both Architect answers, the build brief, the review packet and the handoff.

## Verdict: RETURN

One High finding blocks the merge. Finding 1 is a new honest-state regression. The Dispatch Analysis "What can be concluded" step states a stored result that is not current without saying so. It sits beside a state line that reads "Using the latest checked readings". The rest of the slice holds the honest-state rules well. The fix is small.

## Findings

1. **High: Dispatch Analysis concludes from a stored, non-current version without framing it as historical.**
   - **Where.** `frontend/src/sites/PerformanceDispatch.tsx:1179-1183` (`ReadingsToOutcome` conclusion). The selection comes from `:286-288` (`selected = inWindow ?? loaded`).
   - **What is wrong.**
     - The conclusion is framed only by the day's state: `read.status === "CURRENT" && !read.stale`.
     - When the address selects a stored version that is not the day's current one, `inWindow` is null. The Overview marks this case "pinned" with "This is a stored result that is not the current one" (`:774`). Analysis has no such marking.
   - **What I saw.** I wrote a scratch test from the file's own fixtures and deleted it afterwards. It used `?day=2026-10-02&revision=rev-old&view=analysis`, with `rev-old` an older-rule version and the day CURRENT.
     - The conclusion read: "Possibly avoidable, for the period 03:45 to 04:15 UTC."
     - The state line above it read: "Using the latest checked readings. This result uses every accepted record for the dayâ€¦"
     - `[data-dispatch-pinned]` was absent.
   - **Why it matters.**
     - A superseded, possibly different, outcome reads as the current positive conclusion. That breaks the rule that historical results are framed as historical, and the brief's "no wording may promote â€¦ non-current".
     - The slice makes this path easier to reach. "Versions of this period" moved out of the closed stored-record disclosure into a visible panel (`:1286-1298`), and every link there opens exactly this view.
     - The base had no conclusion sentence, so this is new in this slice.
   - **Fix.**
     - In `ReadingsToOutcome` (or `AnalysisView`), treat `selected` as current only when it is in `read.intervals`.
     - Otherwise frame the conclusion as historical, for example "â€¦, from a stored version that is not the current result". Also show the pinned/older-rule notice in Analysis.
     - Add a test with a pinned version and a CURRENT day, asserting the historical framing and the absence of the bare positive sentence.

2. **Low: the narrow-width claim in `tools/w1-ux-03-evidence.mjs` does not check the viewport width.**
   - **Where.** `tools/w1-ux-03-evidence.mjs:208`. It has the same shape as the shared defect in `w1-ux-01-evidence.mjs`.
   - **What is wrong.** "No sideways scroll" passes on `scrollWidth <= clientWidth` and never asserts that `clientWidth` is near 390. If the override did not hold, the "narrow" claims would silently measure desktop width.
   - **Mitigation I verified.** The run logs in the implementer's scratchpad record the actual widths:
     - `w1ux03-shots-final-disp.txt`, `-ea.txt` and `-stale.txt` show `clientWidth` 390 at narrow and 1425 at desktop.
     - This covers dispatch, analysis and the three evidence-analysis cases, so this run did lay out at 390.
     - No saved log covers the `evidence` screen (the `be9b75b5` pass), so its narrow claims are not established by a recorded value.
     - The packet cites counts ("30/30") but no widths.
   - **Fix.**
     - Add a claim that fails unless `innerWidth === width` and `clientWidth` is within `[width-20, width]`, the shared-script fix.
     - Rerun `evidence` at 390, or record it as not established.
     - Cite the measured widths in the packet.

3. **Low: the Gateway contact fact understates mixed states.**
   - **Where.** `frontend/src/sites/LedgerSiteEvidence.tsx:547`.
   - **What is wrong.** Take one CURRENT gateway and one in another non-STALE state. The fact reads "No gateway report has been received yet", which is false for the CURRENT gateway.
   - **Why it is only Low.** It is non-promoting, with tone `not-known`, but it is inaccurate.
   - **Fix.** Use a neutral mixed sentence, for example "Some gateways are in contact; the others' contact is not known."

4. **Low: prose is treated as an exact value in the S3-02b technical disclosure.**
   - **Where.** `EvidenceClaimLimit.tsx:776` ("Rule wording") and `:809` ("What is not inferred"). The reason list does the same with `${code}: ${text}`.
   - **What is wrong.** Whole server sentences are wrapped in the single-value `source-reference` exemption. `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux-disclosure` allows labelled exact values, not exempted explanations. These are server-recorded strings kept verbatim from S3-02b, so the risk is small. They are, however, unscanned prose.
   - **Fix.** Keep them, but scan them, or record them as an Architect-acknowledged deviation.

## Judgement on the named deviations

- **Deviation 1 (the one pinned sentence containing "claim"): acceptable.**
  - The test allowance (`performanceDispatch.test.tsx`, Analysis W1 scan) is narrow. It filters only phrase `claim` whose text is inside `[data-dispatch-gated]`, and asserts at most one such hit.
  - Rewording would fail the G-04 missing-claim baseline. The open item is in the handoff.
- **Deviation 5 (block links named only by aria-label): acceptable.**
  - Each link's accessible name is plain and exact, for example "04:00 to 04:15 UTC: Possibly avoidable" (tested).
  - The row summary gives the counts in visible text.
  - The existing per-period block chooser below gives a visible-text, keyboard path to the same selection.
- **Deviation 2 ("As of: time not recorded" for no-day and store-not-configured): honest.** The time is never invented. The refused, unavailable and not-found states are bounded by the time the answer arrived. That is labelled in a code comment, not on screen, but it bounds the refusal and nothing else.

## Honest-state checks (what holds)

- **Stale Dispatch.**
  - The headline is prefixed "From an earlier result that is not current:" whenever the status is not CURRENT, or `stale` is set.
  - The totals keep the "not current" pinned line. The state line uses the shared non-positive labels (tested for all four non-current states, including tone).
- **Unstated totals.** `portion()` returns null when any period lacks the key, and the result renders "not known" in both the headline and the cards (tested). `operationText` still counts a missing lower bound as 0. That is a valid lower bound, not a claim of zero.
- **Missing inputs.**

Every changed assertion either still asserts the same fact in its new place, or is stricter. None was weakened.

- **`performanceDispatch.test.tsx` status labels.** They change to the shared labels and add `expectStateLineVisible` plus a non-positive tone check (stronger).
- **Older-rule assertion.** It now asserts that the versions are absent from prose and present, in order, in a closed disclosure. The values are still checked.
- **Fixture `read_at` "x" changed to a real instant.** This change is to test data, not to any assertion.
- **`t028Screens.test.tsx`.**
  - The method version, assessment reference and record-set digest are still asserted, now inside the closed disclosure, plus an `open === false` check.
  - Only the `[data-finding-assessment-id]` selector was dropped. The ID itself is still asserted.
- **`evidenceClaimLimit.test.tsx`.** Two string updates for banned words: "run" became "period of generator operation", and "discharge capability" became "output limit". Same meaning.

# W1-UX-05 â€” Foundation, Readiness, SLD, Health and battery presentation

Status: done (merged to main 2026-10-17; review ACCEPT; owner visual acceptance in the combined W1 walkthrough); was in_review (built 2026-10-17 on `task/W1-UX-05`, both phases; packet `.agent/W1-UX-05-review-packet.md`); activated 2026-10-16 from main `b4a89314`. Scope: `foundation`, `readiness`, `sld`, `health`, `asset`. Use [umbrella](W1-UX-mockup-fidelity.md), v69 named screens/manifest, S1-01/02/03 and S7-01b packets.

## Visible result

Foundation leads with what equipment and operating settings are recorded for the selected period, using a compact equipment/configuration graphic. Readiness leads with what still needs connecting or confirming and a next-step list. SLD keeps the real configured diagram but makes reporting/unknown links and legend readable before part IDs. Health leads with source contact and reported fact age in separate visual lanes; a recent receipt never implies healthy equipment or fresh facts. Battery asset detail prominently says physical condition cannot be judged when the required causal inputs are missing, lists the missing inputs and preserves accepted SOC/rating as separate facts. No warranty, replacement or remaining-life statement.

Initial seams `StoredFoundationViews.tsx:183,367-426,494-675`, `FoundationBindingsPanel.tsx:158`, `ReadinessOverview.tsx:264,347-400,512-570`; S7-01b Health/asset components are merge dependencies. Move exact revision, signal/diagnostic/binding IDs and raw status basis into `DigDeeper`; retain precise selected-time, record and diagram links. Preserve `MissingClaim` for unsupported commissioning, firmware, uptime, signal strength and physical condition. No â€œgreen zeroâ€ for absent reporting. Clearly separate not registered, never received, stale contact, old facts, unavailable read and not configured battery.

Shared acceptance applies to this slice: opt every owned route into the W1 vocabulary set as it migrates; scan default, graphics, accessibility and Analysis/Evidence prose. With the exact-reference disclosure closed, test each applicable non-positive state for a visible plain state sentence, scope and â€œas of <time>â€ outside it. Keep material limitations beside the headline, and substantive depth in the owning contextual tab/view link at 1440 and 390. Preserve labelled exact references, authored/test-data qualification and fail-closed assertions. Capture 1440/390 served app versus `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html#/<id>` using a per-slice Playwright evidence script following `tools/v01a-foundation-evidence.mjs`; record each skipped capture as **not run** with its blocking precondition.

# W1 combined owner walkthrough (draft written at W1-UX-05 closeout, 2026-10-17)

Source: umbrella `tasks/W1-UX-mockup-fidelity.md` "Combined walkthrough coverage and authorship". Owner: whichever UX slice merges last. UX-05 wrote this draft; if UX-02, 03 or 04 merges later, that slice refreshes its rows and captures. The coordinator checks all 26 rows before scheduling the owner session.

## How to run it

- Serve one tool at a time; never serve two at once.
- Substitute only the IDs and selectors that the tool's `links`/`serve` output prints.
- Compare the app with `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html#/<id>` at 1440 and 390 px. Check keyboard access, the exact links and a reload.
- Drop each guarded case afterwards.
- Do not compare numbers across cases.

All data is authored or generated test data, not field equipment readings. Label it that way in the guide and in every screenshot.

### Prerequisites

1. Postgres must be running on 127.0.0.1:55432 (`ASSETOPS_PG_PASSWORD`).
2. Copy the Kobo site file into this worktree: `var/sites/kobo-001.yaml`, taken from the main checkout (`var/` is ignored).
3. Run `python tools/s4_finding_cases.py run` once. It produces the `assetops_s4cases` database and manifest that `v01a`, `v01b` and the S4 tools copy.
4. `s2_demo_walkthrough.py` has `run|serve|setup`, with no `prepare` mode.
   - Use `run`, then `serve`.
   - `run` recreates the fixed database `assetops_s2demo`; drop it afterwards, because the tool has no drop mode.
5. `s5_overview_walkthrough.py prepare` needs `var/sites/kobo-001.yaml`. Run `tick` within 5 minutes before viewing.
6. **Frontend for each tool:** from `frontend/`, set `$env:ASSETOPS_API_PORT="<api>"` and run `npm.cmd run dev -- --port <web> --strictPort`.
   - Vite listens on `localhost`, not `127.0.0.1`.
   - Open the printed path on `http://localhost:<web>`.
7. **Captures:** `node tools/w1-ux-05-evidence.mjs <out> <mock-id>[:<case>]=<url> ...` (or the UX-01, 02, 03 or 04 sibling script for that slice's cards).

5. [W1-UX-05 configuration and health](W1-UX-05-foundation-health.md): `foundation`, `readiness`, `sld`, `health`, `asset`; starts after S7-01b merge.

UX-01 starts only after S6-02b, S6-03 and S7-01b merge. UX-04 is M in two bounded phases; split its second phase if the merged component exceeds that scope. Each packet has at most two phases, focused tests, one full `npm.cmd test` on final revision, 1440/390 served app versus named mock screenshots using per-slice Playwright evidence scripts following `tools/v01a-foundation-evidence.mjs`, with recorded differences; skipped captures are marked **not run** with the blocking precondition, and a fresh reviewer independent of implementation. Keep read/selector/URL/lineage tests and fail-closed assertions; any weakened fail-closed assertion blocks review. Each route opts into the full W1 vocabulary term set as its slice migrates it; all 26 must be opted in before the combined walkthrough. With the exact-reference disclosure closed, tests on every non-positive state assert the plain state sentence, scope and â€œas of <time>â€ remain visible outside it. Test unknown charts as a distinct â€œnot knownâ€ mark, never a zero bar. No new empty/current/positive state without proof from the read. Run `node frontend/docs/architecture/check_v69_dependency_registry.cjs` and relevant `tools/check-all.ps1` guards. Treat unavailable/dirty/withdrawn and the mock's unsupported values as explicit limited states. No per-slice owner walkthrough.

Questions answered by `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux` in `.agent/architect-W1-ux-answers.md`. let viewport = [1440, 1000];
async function shot(name) {
  // The app shell scrolls inside its own frame, so grow the viewport to the tallest scroller for the capture.
  const tall = await js(`Math.max(document.documentElement.scrollHeight,
    ...[...document.querySelectorAll('*')].filter(e => e.scrollHeight > e.clientHeight + 1 && getComputedStyle(e).overflowY !== 'visible')
      .map(e => e.scrollHeight + e.getBoundingClientRect().top))`);
  const h = Math.min(Math.ceil(tall), 7000);
  await send("Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride", { width: viewport[0], height: h, deviceScaleFactor: 1, mobile: viewport[0] < 600 });
  await sleep(300);
  const r = await send("Page.captureScreenshot", { format: "png" });
  writeFileSync(join(OUT, `${name}.png`), Buffer.from(r.data, "base64"));
  await size(viewport[0], viewport[1]);
}

    not URL, reason="ASSETOPS_TEST_DATABASE_URL is not set (see backend/README-db.md)"
)

if URL:
    from fastapi import FastAPI
    from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
    from test_s1_bindings_postgres import NOW, Clock, engine, store  # noqa: F401
    from test_s7_asset_boundary_postgres import without_battery
    from test_s7_source_health_postgres import published, rig  # noqa: F401

    from assetops_backend.asset_boundary_api import build_asset_boundary_router
    from assetops_backend.sites.cutover import ImportedSiteRegistry
    from assetops_backend.sites.foundation_service import FoundationService

FIXTURE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "fixtures" / "s7_health_api_contract.json"
_UUID = re.compile(r"[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}")
_HEX64 = re.compile(r"\b[0-9a-f]{64}\b")


def normalise(document: dict) -> dict:
    """Stable placeholders for per-run UUIDs and digests, numbered by first appearance."""
    text = json.dumps(document, indent=1, sort_keys=True)
    ids: dict[str, str] = {}
    text = _UUID.sub(lambda m: ids.setdefault(m.group(0), f"00000000-0000-4000-8000-{len(ids) + 1:012d}"), text)
    digests: dict[str, str] = {}
    text = _HEX64.sub(lambda m: digests.setdefault(m.group(0), f"{len(digests) + 1:064x}"), text)
    return json.loads(text)


def answer(response) -> dict:
    return {"status": response.status_code, "body": response.json()}


def test_s7_health_api_contract(store, rig, published, tmp_path) -> None:  # noqa: F811
    without_battery(store, tmp_path, code="KOBO-NB")
    health = rig.client()
    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(build_asset_boundary_router(ImportedSiteRegistry(FoundationService(store, "demo", Clock(NOW)))))
    asset = TestClient(app)

    class Down:
        def source_health_snapshot(self, tenant_id, site_id):
            from assetops_backend.evidence.ledger import LedgerUnavailable
            raise LedgerUnavailable("store down")

    entry_001 = asset.get("/api/sites/KOBO-001/battery")
    entry_002 = asset.get("/api/sites/KOBO-002/battery")
    kobo_001_asset = entry_001.json()["batteries"][0]["asset_id"]
    contract = {
        "health_kobo001": answer(health.get("/api/sites/KOBO-001/source-health")),
        "health_kobo002": answer(health.get("/api/sites/KOBO-002/source-health")),
        "health_unknown_site": answer(health.get("/api/sites/NOT-A-SITE/source-health")),
        "health_unavailable": answer(rig.client(store=Down()).get("/api/sites/KOBO-001/source-health")),
        "battery_entry_kobo001": answer(entry_001),
        "battery_detail_kobo001": answer(asset.get(entry_001.json()["batteries"][0]["detail_path"])),
        "battery_entry_kobo002": answer(entry_002),
        "battery_detail_kobo002": answer(asset.get(entry_002.json()["batteries"][0]["detail_path"])),
        "battery_entry_kobonb": answer(asset.get("/api/sites/KOBO-NB/battery")),
        "battery_detail_foreign": answer(asset.get(f"/api/sites/KOBO-NB/assets/{kobo_001_asset}/battery")),
        "battery_detail_invalid": answer(asset.get("/api/sites/KOBO-001/assets/not-an-id/battery")),
    }
    c = contract
    assert c["health_kobo001"]["body"]["summary"]["status"] == "NOT_ALL_CURRENT"
    assert c["health_kobo002"]["body"]["summary"]["reason"] == "NO_ENABLED_GATEWAY"
    assert c["health_unknown_site"]["status"] == 404
    assert c["health_unavailable"]["status"] == 503
    assert c["battery_detail_kobo001"]["body"]["binding_status"] == "BOUND"
    assert c["battery_detail_kobo002"]["body"]["binding_status"] == "NO_BINDING_SET"
    assert c["battery_entry_kobonb"]["body"]["configuration"] == "NOT_CONFIGURED"
    assert c["battery_detail_foreign"]["status"] == 404
    assert c["battery_detail_invalid"]["status"] == 422
    if os.environ.get("ASSETOPS_WRITE_S7_CONTRACT"):
        FIXTURE.write_text(json.dumps(normalise(contract), indent=1, sort_keys=True) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    committed = json.loads(FIXTURE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert normalise(contract) == committed

+# W1-UX-06 — W1 presentation closeout and combined owner walkthrough
+
+Status: **implementation-ready**. Size **S–M**, at most two phases. Implement on `task/W1-UX-06` after W1-UX-02, -03 and -04 are reviewed and merged; this packet is written on `plan/W1-UX-06`. Coordinator commits the plan. This is the last W1 slice before the owner's single combined walkthrough under `.agent/owner-walkthrough-policy-2026-10-16.md`. Fresh independent slice review gates merge; the owner walks the combined result once and W1 closes only after that acceptance. No new panel claim or source promotion.
+
+## Result and boundaries
+
+The final walkthrough measures the actual 1440 and 390 px layout of all **26 named Site screens**, plus S4-08 assumption history states, with labelled authored/generated/simulated data. It gives a non-developer one ordered script: prerequisites, exact PowerShell commands, private database/work names, ports, tool-printed URLs, state to inspect, keyboard/link/reload checks, app-versus-v69 screenshots, and cleanup. Never invent fixture IDs or compare values across unrelated cases. Unsupported or refused states stay visibly limited; a missing capture is recorded **not run** with the blocking precondition, never a pass.
+

+## Architect questions (recommendations; settle before wording changes)
+
+1. May the S1 Readiness badge `Ready to report` become **Set up to report** (retaining its configured tone), matching the neutral card and explicitly meaning configuration only? Recommend yes.
+2. Should the Readiness strip title `From configuration to confirmed reporting` become **Set-up steps** while stale gateway contact stays in the separate Gateway status panel, or should the strip gain a health read? Recommend the title change: the strip has no health input and must not suggest current contact.
+3. When the selected gateway is in contact but its latest reported facts are old, should its state line say **In contact; its latest report is old**? Recommend yes, with contact and fact age kept distinct and the limitation visible outside details.
+4. For unrecognised readiness and health codes, may the two optimistic headlines (`Nothing is missing from the set-up`; `All 0 enabled gateways are in contact...`) fall back to **Set-up state not recognised** and **Gateway contact cannot be confirmed**? Recommend yes; zero enabled gateways also takes the non-positive branch. Keep next steps and exact codes available.
+
+Record the Architect's ruling in `.agent/` and implement only the approved copy, preserving the response meaning and tests. No answer is needed to fix measurement, shared helper, backend lag or backend wording.

+## Phases and acceptance

+# W1-UX-06 — W1 presentation closeout and combined owner walkthrough
+
+Status: **implementation-ready**. Size **S–M**, at most two phases. Implement on `task/W1-UX-06` after W1-UX-02, -03 and -04 are reviewed and merged; this packet is written on `plan/W1-UX-06`. Coordinator commits the plan. This is the last W1 slice before the owner's single combined walkthrough under `.agent/owner-walkthrough-policy-2026-10-16.md`. Fresh independent slice review gates merge; the owner walks the combined result once and W1 closes only after that acceptance. No new panel claim or source promotion.
+
+## Result and boundaries
+
+The final walkthrough measures the actual 1440 and 390 px layout of all **26 named Site screens**, plus S4-08 assumption history states, with labelled authored/generated/simulated data. It gives a non-developer one ordered script: prerequisites, exact PowerShell commands, private database/work names, ports, tool-printed URLs, state to inspect, keyboard/link/reload checks, app-versus-v69 screenshots, and cleanup. Never invent fixture IDs or compare values across unrelated cases. Unsupported or refused states stay visibly limited; a missing capture is recorded **not run** with the blocking precondition, never a pass.
+

+## Architect questions (recommendations; settle before wording changes)
+
+1. May the S1 Readiness badge `Ready to report` become **Set up to report** (retaining its configured tone), matching the neutral card and explicitly meaning configuration only? Recommend yes.
+2. Should the Readiness strip title `From configuration to confirmed reporting` become **Set-up steps** while stale gateway contact stays in the separate Gateway status panel, or should the strip gain a health read? Recommend the title change: the strip has no health input and must not suggest current contact.
+3. When the selected gateway is in contact but its latest reported facts are old, should its state line say **In contact; its latest report is old**? Recommend yes, with contact and fact age kept distinct and the limitation visible outside details.
+4. For unrecognised readiness and health codes, may the two optimistic headlines (`Nothing is missing from the set-up`; `All 0 enabled gateways are in contact...`) fall back to **Set-up state not recognised** and **Gateway contact cannot be confirmed**? Recommend yes; zero enabled gateways also takes the non-positive branch. Keep next steps and exact codes available.
+
+Record the Architect's ruling in `.agent/` and implement only the approved copy, preserving the response meaning and tests. No answer is needed to fix measurement, shared helper, backend lag or backend wording.

+# W1-UX-06 — W1 presentation closeout and combined owner walkthrough
+
+Status: **implementation-ready**. Size **S–M**, at most two phases. Implement on `task/W1-UX-06` after W1-UX-02, -03 and -04 are reviewed and merged; this packet is written on `plan/W1-UX-06`. Coordinator commits the plan. This is the last W1 slice before the owner's single combined walkthrough under `.agent/owner-walkthrough-policy-2026-10-16.md`. Fresh independent slice review gates merge; the owner walks the combined result once and W1 closes only after that acceptance. No new panel claim or source promotion.
+
+## Result and boundaries
+
+The final walkthrough measures the actual 1440 and 390 px layout of all **26 named Site screens**, plus S4-08 assumption history states, with labelled authored/generated/simulated data. It gives a non-developer one ordered script: prerequisites, exact PowerShell commands, private database/work names, ports, tool-printed URLs, state to inspect, keyboard/link/reload checks, app-versus-v69 screenshots, and cleanup. Never invent fixture IDs or compare values across unrelated cases. Unsupported or refused states stay visibly limited; a missing capture is recorded **not run** with the blocking precondition, never a pass.
+
+### Code reality and review carries (current branch, before dependencies merge)
+
+| Seam | Current fact / implementation boundary |
+| --- | --- |
+| `tools/w1-ux-01-evidence.mjs:99-114,130-141,184-186` and `tools/w1-ux-05-evidence.mjs:98-113,130-141,184-186` | Device metrics are set, but the overflow and visible-state claims do not prove the requested width. UX-05 review L1 observed `clientWidth=1425` in a nominal 390 pass. `tools/v01a-foundation-evidence.mjs:107-135,164,209,250` has the same width-risk pattern. +## Architect questions (recommendations; settle before wording changes)
+
+1. May the S1 Readiness badge `Ready to report` become **Set up to report** (retaining its configured tone), matching the neutral card and explicitly meaning configuration only? Recommend yes.
+2. Should the Readiness strip title `From configuration to confirmed reporting` become **Set-up steps** while stale gateway contact stays in the separate Gateway status panel, or should the strip gain a health read? Recommend the title change: the strip has no health input and must not suggest current contact.
+3. When the selected gateway is in contact but its latest reported facts are old, should its state line say **In contact; its latest report is old**? Recommend yes, with contact and fact age kept distinct and the limitation visible outside details.
+4. For unrecognised readiness and health codes, may the two optimistic headlines (`Nothing is missing from the set-up`; `All 0 enabled gateways are in contact...`) fall back to **Set-up state not recognised** and **Gateway contact cannot be confirmed**? Recommend yes; zero enabled gateways also takes the non-positive branch. Keep next steps and exact codes available.
+
+Record the Architect's ruling in `.agent/` and implement only the approved copy, preserving the response meaning and tests. No answer is needed to fix measurement, shared helper, backend lag or backend wording.

+# W1-UX-06 — W1 presentation closeout and combined owner walkthrough
+
+Status: **implementation-ready**. Size **S–M**, at most two phases. Implement on `task/W1-UX-06` after W1-UX-02, -03 and -04 are reviewed and merged; this packet is written on `plan/W1-UX-06`. Coordinator commits the plan. This is the last W1 slice before the owner's single combined walkthrough under `.agent/owner-walkthrough-policy-2026-10-16.md`. Fresh independent slice review gates merge; the owner walks the combined result once and W1 closes only after that acceptance. No new panel claim or source promotion.
+
+## Result and boundaries
+
+The final walkthrough measures the actual 1440 and 390 px layout of all **26 named Site screens**, plus S4-08 assumption history states, with labelled authored/generated/simulated data. It gives a non-developer one ordered script: prerequisites, exact PowerShell commands, private database/work names, ports, tool-printed URLs, state to inspect, keyboard/link/reload checks, app-versus-v69 screenshots, and cleanup. Never invent fixture IDs or compare values across unrelated cases. Unsupported or refused states stay visibly limited; a missing capture is recorded **not run** with the blocking precondition, never a pass.
